
============================================================
COMPUTING METRICS
============================================================

--- CBR-IDB ---
give me the number of patients who have been diagnosed with tobacco use disorder.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = "Tobacco Use Disorder"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 'tobacco use disorder']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Tobacco use disorder"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'tobacco use disorder']]}
----------

what number of married patients suffered from other convulsions?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."MARITAL_STATUS" = "MARRIED" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = "Other convulsions"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'other convulsions'], ['demographic."marital_status"', '=', 'married']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."MARITAL_STATUS" = "MARRIED" AND DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = "Other convulsions"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."marital_status"', '=', 'married'], ['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 'other convulsions']]}

provide the number of patients whose death status is 0 and primary disease is aortic insufficiency/re-do sternotomy; aortic valve replacement.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DEATH_STATUS" = "0" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = "AORTIC INSUFFICIENCY\RE-DO STERNOTOMY; AORTIC VALVE REPLACEMENT"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."death_status"', '=', '0'], ['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'aortic insufficiency\\re-do sternotomy; aortic valve replacement']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."EXPIRE_FLAG" = "0" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = "AORTIC INSUFFICIENCY\RE-DO STERNOTOMY; AORTIC VALVE REPLACEMENT"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'aortic insufficiency\\re-do sternotomy; aortic valve replacement'], ['demographic."expire_flag"', '=', '0']]}
----------

provide the number of patients with a diagnoses of personal history of other malignant neoplasm of rectum, rectosigmoid junction and anus and are still alive.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = 'Personal history of malignant neoplasm of rectum, rectosigmoid junction, and anus' AND DEMOGRAPHIC."AGE" > 0
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."age"', '>', '0'], ['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 'personal history of malignant neoplasm of rectum, rectosigmoid junction,']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."EXPIRE_FLAG" = "0" AND DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Hx-rectal & anal malign"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."expire_flag"', '=', '0'], ['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'hx-rectal & anal malign']]}
----------

How many patients were admitted through the emergency department and were discharged to discharge-transitioning to psychiatric hospital?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_TYPE" = "EMERGENCY" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."DISCHARGE_LOCATION" = "SHORT TERM HOSPITAL"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."admission_type"', '=', 'emergency'], ['demographic."discharge_location"', '=', 'short term hospital']]}
----------

SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID WHERE PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG_TYPE" = "MAIN" AND LAB."LABEL" = "Sodium, Urine"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'prescriptions', 'lab'], 'cond': [['lab."label"', '=', 'sodium, urine'], ['prescriptions."drug_type"', '=', 'main']]}

{'sel': '', 'agg': ['demographic."age"', 'diagnoses."short_title"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['demographic."subject_id"', '=', '4589']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT DEMOGRAPHIC."AGE",DEMOGRAPHIC."EXPIRE_FLAG" FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" = "4589"
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['demographic."age"', 'demographic."expire_flag"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."subject_id"', '=', '4589']]}

----------

count the number of patients who have stayed in the hospital for more than 23 days with a drug phenylephrine hcl.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DAYS_STAY" > "23" AND PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG" = "Phenylephrine HCl"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['demographic."days_stay"', '>', '23'], ['prescriptions."drug"', '=', 'phenylephrine hcl']]}
----------
TRUTH:

SELECT DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMITTIME",DEMOGRAPHIC."DAYS_STAY" FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."NAME" = "Michael Witt"
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['demographic."admittime"', 'demographic."days_stay"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."name"', '=', 'michael witt']]}
----------

----------
PREDS:

{'sel': '', 'agg': [], 'tab': ['demographic', 'procedures'], 'cond': [['demographic."name"', '=', 'dominga garvin']]}
----------

SELECT PROCEDURES."LONG_TITLE" FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PROCEDURES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."NAME" = "Dominga Garvin"
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['procedures."long_title"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'procedures'], 'cond': [['demographic."name"', '=', 'dominga garvin']]}

how many of the unmarried patients belonged to white ethnic origin?
----------

SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_TYPE" = "URGENT" AND PRESCRIPTIONS."ROUTE" = "REPLACE"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['demographic."admission_type"', '=', 'urgent'], ['prescriptions."route"', '=', 'replace']]}

SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ETHNICITY" = "ASIAN"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['demographic."ethnicity"', '=', 'asian']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ETHNICITY" = "ASIAN" AND PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG_TYPE" = "BASE"

GOLD: SELECT DEMOGRAPHIC."DISCHARGE_LOCATION",DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."NAME" = "James Sloan"

GOLD: SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."INSURANCE" = "Self Pay" AND DIAGNOSES."ICD9_CODE" = "430"
PRED_OUT: Counter({(0,): 1})
GOLD_OUT: Counter({(13,): 1})
